# Rig revamp #2 ledger

Paths in entries before 2026-10-22 are relative to `.git/revamp/`, now `revamp/` beside this file.

## North Star (AGENTS.md)
Rig is a fast, indentation-sensitive systems language that compiles to Zig.
A small algebra of sigils makes ownership visible where it happens. Readability
of Python/Ruby, cost model of Zig/C, safety of Rust: no GC, no hidden
allocation, no hidden refcount traffic, no silent control flow. Accept means
correct; safe code cannot corrupt memory; effects stay visible in the IR.

## Inputs (user answered 2026-09-25)

  docs; close small completeness gaps (break-with-value, field defaults,
  module-level constants, slices). Generic fns/traits/stack closure args stay
  on the ROADMAP.
- `(!xs).push(i)` explicit write borrow rule: KEEP (fixed).

## Phase 2 plan
Whole at once (tightly coupled compiler passes, ~20k lines). Audit (8 agents, 2026-09-25):
frontend (grammar, rig.zig, diag, modules, main, build) | sema (sema.zig, resolve.zig) |
typecheck (typecheck.zig, effects.zig) | ownership | emit (emit.zig, runtime.zig) |
fuzz (black-box adversarial) | docs (docs, tests, runner, build) | gaps (completeness design).
Audit files: .git/revamp/audit-<name>.md

## Phase 4 triage (2026-09-25)
Spot-checked (reproduced on base): O1 (double free), T1 (5.0/2.0 prints 2), O2 (defer after return: panic), F1 (emit panic).
Verdict FIX for every critical/high in audit-{frontend,sema,typecheck,ownership,emit,docs,gaps}.md unless listed below.

Rejected / deferred:
- F36 (compiler on gpa instead of arena): REJECT. Batch compiler; arena is intentional and fast.
- Keyword-as-member-name, `**T` double sigil, String match, struct ==: DEFER (roadmap; feature work).
- Lifting array/Vec owning-element limits, owned closures with non-Copy params, drop on enums/generics,
  imported generic instances (S1 long-term): DEFER (roadmap). S1 gets the transitive check + importType guard.
- `try` value blocks, `pre` expressions: removed from grammar, keywords stay reserved (F32), roadmap keeps them.
- Bound method refs `p.get`: keep rejected (hidden receiver capture).
- EM-21 --release=fast UB on overflow: document as the one mode outside the overflow guarantee (bounds checks stay explicit in runtime).
- D2 docs/zig-0.16.md: TRIM (not 120 lines): keep Zig 0.16 API reference relevant to the compiler, runtime,
  emitted code and near-term stdlib work; drop migration narrative, AI bootstrap prompt, chat artifacts, pre-0.15 material.

Accepted restriction removals (behavior changes to call out):
- S13 drop "pub fun may not mention a private type" (inconsistent; leaking via fields already works).
- S25 drop "drop body cannot reassign resource field" (redundant/unsound-order); keep `self = ...` rule.
- T12 binary shift with different-width amount (SPEC already says so).
- T13/F19 single-quoted strings take no escapes (SPEC already says so).
- T25 float division by constant 0 allowed.
- T20/T17/T18/T19/T26 borrow/optional/literal unify gaps: small, accept.
- EM-27 floats print with a decimal point (1.0) — behavior change.
- F20 test names in either quote style. Trailing commas in lists (if no grammar conflicts).
- New safeguards: O5 values stored via Cell.set/replace/Signal.subscribe may not carry borrows;
  O6 cannot clone a value holding a write loan; F9 `x =!y` rejected; F10 touching operands rejected;
  F3 root file must end in .rig; F6 main must be `sub main()`; F4 lowercase radix/no leading zeros.

Gap features (phase C, sequential, one agent): nested closure capture, field defaults, Type.method values,
fn-typed field calls, module-level constants (=!), String slicing + ?xs[a..b] array slices, `e?`, break-with-value.

## Phase 5 plan
A (structural, worktree, alone): F32 remove reserved syntax; fold effects.zig into typecheck; BindingKind (F33).
B (parallel worktrees, disjoint files): front (grammar, rig.zig, diag, modules, main, build, test/run) |
  sema (sema.zig, resolve.zig) | typecheck (typecheck.zig) | ownership | emit (emit.zig, runtime.zig).
C gaps (sequential). D docs. E review.

Fuzz (audit-fuzz.md, ~5,300 programs) new items -> owners:
 FZ-F9 for over owning temporary leaks (emit) | FZ-F10 drop recursion via indirect `self =` (resolve: forbid passing whole `self` as write borrow in drop body)
 FZ-F11 `return` inside defer (typecheck) | FZ-F12 bare local statement `x` -> pointless discard (emit) | FZ-F3 nested same label (emit/typecheck)
 FZ-F2 unused generic param (emit) | FZ-F14 drop queue LIFO order (runtime, = EM-28) | FZ-F15 quadratic ownership name lookup (ownership)
 FZ-F7/F8 payload moves out of fields/captures (ownership, = O3 + captured) | FZ-F6 (= O9) | FZ-F1/F4/F5/F13 dup of S6/T7/S4/EM-7.

## Phase: syntax consistency (user-approved 2026-09-25, before SYNTAX.md)
1 Vec .len (drop length()) | 2 catch-all arms: `_` and names; drop `else =>` | 3 Cell: drop c.value, keep get() |
4 payload variants: keyword args required | 5 fn types `fun(A) -> R` | 6 no paren-free calls inside ( ) |
7 remove `extern name: fun(...)` form (extern fun/sub only; extern name: T for data) |
8 error on local assigned but never read (locals only; drop-glue values count as used; `_ = e` escape) |
9 keywords allowed as field/method names and after `.` / as kwarg names.
SYNTAX.md draft parked at scratchpad/syn/SYNTAX.draft.md.
- DECISION (user): keep `=!` for fixed bindings; mnemonic "set, dammit!" (use in SYNTAX.md bindings section).
- USER (2026-09-25): authorized: land 9 changes + SYNTAX.md, push revamp, PR, merge (CI on Linux). Cell(Vec) forwarding methods NOT approved now (deferred; add to ROADMAP).
- USER: Cell(Vec) APPROVED after all. Cell(Vec(T)) answers Vec members: push, pop, clear, len (field), [i]/get(i)/[i]=x Copy-only; atomic in runtime (clear empties then drops). Queue after items 1-9, before SYNTAX.md.
- Added: statement-must-have-use rule (e2e9c6e), paren-less decl style in docs (6194fe2), NaN print + CI timeout (67482fb, dfb95da). CI green at dfb95da; rerunning.
- USER DECISION: keep e! (propagate error) and e? (propagate none): suffix matches type suffix. No change.
- USER DECISION (2026-09-25): ADOPT receiver-mode sigils `!P.m(args)` = `(!P).m(args)`, `<P.m(args)` = `(<P).m(args)`, WITH guardrails:
  (1) `!` on a Bool context -> "`!` is a write borrow; use `not`" (also fixes bug: `b = !a` printed true; `if !a` Zig error)
  (2) `!` before a method that doesn't take !self -> error, hint `not`; `<` before a method not taking self: Self -> error
  (3) short form rejected when the call's value is Bool; write `(!P).m(args)`
  Keep `and/or/not` words. Also fix: `k = +root.first()` (clone of returned handle) -> Zig error.
- Designer review: prototype green (0 conflicts, 1186/1186, 299 sites converted) at scratchpad/decide/designer/proto. Paren-free + while step need 4 grammar lines. Head-of-chain place rule. Extra bug: grow(!make()) write borrow of temp. Forwarded to implementer; kept owner's stricter Bool guardrail (c).
- TODO next push (user-approved): ROADMAP line: private fields (module-private unless pub) so setter methods can guard invariants; the tool for encapsulation, not properties.
- TODO next push: SYNTAX.md gaps: (1) e!/e? rationale (suffix matches type), (2) fields vs properties consequence + fields public today + private fields roadmap, (3) NaN prints nan. RULE: every language change updates SYNTAX.md in the same commit.
- USER DECISION (2026-09-25): ADOPT square brackets for ALL compile-time params: `type Box[T]`, `Vec[Int]`, `fun max[T](a: T, b: T)`, `fun check[mode: Mode](n)`, `check[.strict](5)`, `max[Float](1,2)`; remove `pre` keyword; ADD generic functions. Update ALL docs incl SYNTAX.md.
  PLAN: finish PR #5 (receiver sigils + doc gaps + roadmap private fields) -> merge; new branch `brackets` -> phases: prototype, migration, generic fns, adversarial review, docs -> PR #6.

## Brackets (PR #6), branch `brackets` from main @ 6df0c48 (PR #5 merged 2026-09-25; repo allow_auto_merge enabled per user)
- USER (2026-09-25): brackets REPLACE `pre` entirely: compile-time value params move into the bracket list with type params. `fun check[mode: Mode](n: Int)`, `check[.strict](5)`; bare name in brackets = type param; `name: T` = compile-time value. `pre` is no longer a keyword.
- Lead decisions: expression-position `f[args]` parses as index / multi-arg list; sema decides instantiation vs indexing by what the object names (Go approach). Type args in expression position are expressions read as types (name, a.B, *T, ~T, T?, T!, X[Y]); `[]T`, `[N]T`, `fun(..)` there need an annotation or alias (clear diagnostic). Generic fns: callable only, not values (as pre fns today). No compatibility shims for old syntax.
- Phases: A (agent) grammar + sema + emit + full migration, generic fns still rejected; B generic fns; C adversarial review; D docs (SYNTAX generics section, SPEC, DESIGN, INTERNALS, ROADMAP).

## REVAMP-2 (codebase-revamp skill), started 2026-09-28
North Star (AGENTS.md): Rig is a fast, indentation-sensitive systems language compiling to Zig whose small algebra of sigils makes ownership visible where it happens; readability of Python/Ruby, cost model of Zig/C, safety of Rust: no GC, no hidden allocation/refcount/failure/control flow. Accept means correct; safe code cannot corrupt memory; effects stay visible (sigils are IR nodes). USER GUARDRAIL: language surface FROZEN — changes to what Rig accepts/means go to the user as questions.
Branch `revamp` from main 5f2bdbc (PR #23 merge).
BASELINE: build 0 warnings, ~7s debug; bin/rig 8.8M (debug); tests 1671/0/0 (168s cold, ~55s warm); zig build test ok.
Size: hand-written src 29,490 lines (10 files: typecheck 8169, emit 5367, sema 4560, ownership 4194, resolve 2266, rig 2039, runtime 1807, main 465, modules 332, diag 291); generated parser.zig 5450 + rig.grammar 886; tests 1505 files 32,498 lines; docs 13 md 11,900 lines; examples 417 lines.
Perf: ReleaseFast `rig check` of 580 behavior/example files 1.38s wall incl. process spawn (checker not a bottleneck).
Known issues carried in: see squirrelly list polish items 13-19 (new x: U8 = 5; closure return annotation; fun() type parens; ?F alias callable; raw as binding rhs; use m as n; empty loop body), 20-22 diagnostic hints (def/elif/class/switch habits, did-you-mean, v.len()/True/self.age/x as U8/write ?x), crash traces into generated Zig, deferred LOWs from reviews (sub(Int)! suffix hints, extern sub f()!, closure-in-Vec[*T?], ?? N(...) on owning optional, match E.a =>, generic ?T? as copies (M2), write cursor while !cur as n).
Phase 2 plan: do all at once (tightly coupled compiler; layers share facts), but implementation workstreams by file ownership: FE (rig.zig, grammar, diag, modules, main), SEMA (resolve, sema), TC (typecheck), OWN (ownership), EMIT (emit, runtime), DOCS (*.md), TESTS (test/, build, CI). Structural dedups first, alone.

- VOCABULARY (user, 2026-10-02): lend / view / loan — "You lend a view; the compiler remembers the loan." (in CORE.md via #34). Rationale for docs/DESIGN.md in the SPEC/doc rewrite: the sigil is written on the OWNER's side (`print(?v)`, `grow(!v)`), so the verb belongs to the owner: v *lends*. Rust writes `&v` at the same spot but names it "borrow" (the receiver's view), which is why "borrow" ends up meaning act, reference, and checker record at once. Rig: lend = what the owner does (where the sigil appears); view = what crosses over and travels; loan = what stays with the owner and constrains it. Error messages adopt it: "cannot lend `t` to write while a read loan is live". "Borrow" survives only as a once-mentioned synonym.
- QUEUED (user, 2026-10-02): VOCABULARY PASS — rewrite ALL "borrow" references to lend/view/loan: docs (~365), diagnostics (156 strings) + matching test expectations (870 lines/401 files incl corpus), comments, AND internal identifiers (borrow_read->view_read, borrow_write->view_write, peelBorrows->peelViews, unwrapBorrows, mayCarryBorrow, Borrows, isPtrBorrowTy, ReadBorrow, etc.), grammar (6) + parser regen. By meaning: act->lend ("cannot lend `v` to write"), value->view, record->loan ("while a read loan is live"), borrowed->lent, borrow checker->ownership checker. Only sanctioned "borrow": CORE's note that Rust calls it a borrow. Add a test/lint that fails on stray "borrow". Run AFTER text-core lands (one-branch rule). Then SPEC rewrite.
- ORDER (user, 2026-10-02: "its own PR" + "get everything done first"): vocabulary pass is ONE dedicated PR, run AFTER the code-changing consolidation steps settle (strings land, type facts, classifier+exitTo, lend table, containers, small decisions, static/provenance), immediately BEFORE the SPEC rewrite, so the rename happens once over stable code and SPEC is written in the new vocabulary.

Rig: a fast, indentation-sensitive systems language compiling to Zig; a small algebra of sigils makes ownership visible where it happens. Python/Ruby readability, Zig/C cost model, Rust safety: no GC, no hidden allocation/refcount traffic/control flow. docs/CORE.md is fixed too.

## Design answers (Rig session, 2026-10-03) — all my recs agreed
1 `struct T unique` after name, generic structs yes, enums inferred; Nexus re-sync same change. 2 Cell-holder loop elems/read payloads -> views (step1). 3 Cell admission keyed on moves; owner API = set/replace/take only, no new forwarding. 4 by-value Cell set -> step 3. 5 every made value in header is taken (positive list). 6 +e reads e. 7 unwritten read lend only where view lasts for the use (args, for, as, match, receivers); bindings/fields always written -> reword CORE sentence 4 (precise text). 8 ?o of S? stays ?(S?); (?S)? and String? lend targets. 9 made value lent to view arg = statement temp. 10 *T loan on handle binding; !h lends handle. 11 v.get(i) copy-only + hint. 12 CORE §5 reword: "swap and sort_by work for any element; sort needs <". 13 bare held write view on RHS writes through (local+field). 14 only names whose own scope ends move bare; outer needs <x; update CORE sentence 1 wording. 15 const x = e / const x: T = e; module-level rejected; new const x = e (schema tag); new x: T = e; =! token always errors w/ hint. 16 error values only in branch leaves of return operand. 17 add pending blocks for Bool long form + sentence 7 narrowing; update built/planned words as rules land; fix sentence 1 stale clause now; rule WORDING changes go past Rig first. 18 static param summaries = consolidation: error at call site + note to callee store line; summaries exported cross-module; function value with requirement can't pass as plain ?fun; produces refinement OK. 19 os.env out.
- Rig prefers sema dump as `check --facts=sema` (done: 485e1a06 on revamp, with test/equiv.py).

- committee 4/5 (Zig codegen) forwarded raw. GENERAL RIG COSTS (my domain, any program): inlining.zig resolves method calls by NAME (same-named wrappers look recursive -> never inlined); List.at double bounds check + not inline + record copy; ReleaseSafe stack canaries (-fno-stack-protector?); free poisons 0xAA in release; rarely calls should be cold+noinline. Est Rig 8-15%, Trig 20-30%

## 2026-10-16 riff 2: no-backslash ideas (5 experts, no files)
3 of 5 (shell hacker, Lisp/Elm, systems) favour juxtaposition: print("hello, " name "!"), trailing = shows (warn(price * qty=, tax=)), wrap (price * qty=). 2 of 5 (typographer, APL) favour $"hello, {name}!" ($ is an invalid char today); APL adds postfix $ wrap x$. All pass the hard test (probed). Owner sketch \({e} = @) answered by me. Waiting on owner.

## 2026-10-16 #258 assignthrough Core §6 text decided
4 experts + final pass: "A `!T` a call returns, or that the call's `!` or `?` gives, is a place too: assigning to it writes through, as it would through a binding of it. The call finds the place, so it runs after the value, as an index does." Exclusion sentence dropped (SPEC keeps it). Sent to the round. #258 green (12,863 matrix, oracle silent); waits on #251. cli/markers flake on #249's main CI was an LLVM broken pipe; passes. widemasks branch+worktree cleaned. Templates: owner dislikes all spellings so far; considering backtick templates (JS shape: str, var, str…). Parked; warn/panic can proceed independently.

## 2026-10-17 — hash.Map/Set plan (owner approved)
1. Order: the current round (NEEDS 42, beat Trig's map) lands first. Then one Opus round on a cross-language benchmark (bench/hash/: short/long text and int keys; n = 8, 40, 1k, 100k, 1M; build, hit, miss, delete, walk; Rust indexmap/hashbrown, Zig ArrayHashMap/HashMap, Abseil flat_hash_map, Go map; Mac + pup, plus a guard in the deep gate) and on the winning designs (indexmap layout, group probing on SWAR, full hash kept with each entry, index width sized to the map, fast hashes). The List.at inlining and bounds-proof round runs alongside it.
2. Target: within **5%** of the fastest competitor on each operation, on both machines, or the report explains the gap. The owner hopes we're 5–10% faster.
3. One ordered map; no unordered type unless a measurement shows it's needed.

## 2026-10-17 — #287 std.bigint review (r3/bigint-review.md)
Verdict: merge after a doc fix, which is now done (86cfe8704): "zero allocates nothing" was false; the README now says the port's add and shl were written for the bench, and that the arm64 shl 1L row is noisy.
